A canonical tree-of-tangles theorem for structurally submodular separation systems
Christian Elbracht, Jakob Kneip

Abstract
We show that every structurally submodular separation system admits a canonical tree set which distinguishes its tangles.



1
Introduction

The concept of
tangles
has its origins in the graph minors project of Robertson and Seymour
[
6
]
, where tangles were introduced as a unifying framework with which to describe and study highly cohesive substructures in graphs. A central theorem in their work is a
tree-of-tangles theorem
, which roughly says that the tangles of a graph give rise to a tree-decomposition of that graph with each tangle in a different bag.

Since their inception the theory of tangles has seen a number of advancements: it has been discovered
[
4
,
3
]
that the notion of tangles can be formulated more abstractly and does not require an underlying graph structure, making it applicable to a wider range of combinatorial structures. A
separation system
in this abstract set-up is an axiomatisation of the properties of separations of well-known structures such as graphs or matroids: it is a set, whose elements we call
separations
, that is equipped with a partial ordering in terms of which all the properties important for tangle theory, such as ‘nested’, ‘orientation’, or ‘consistency’, can be expressed
[
4
]
.

This higher level of abstraction, and of stripping away the superfluous information about the underlying graph, have facilitated a number of cleaner proofs and stronger results. A recent result
[
3
]
of Diestel, Hundertmark, and Lemanczyk extends the tree-of-tangles theorem of Robertson and Seymour
[
6
]
to tangles outside graph theory by finding a
tree set
, a set of pairwise nested separations, and in addition to this achieves a significant strengthening: the tree set found in
[
3
]
can be built canonically. The latter means that the construction of the tree set can be carried out using only invariants of the given combinatorial structure. Having a canonical way of constructing the tree set is desirable, for instance, for reproducibility of results when implementing an algorithm for this construction: the canonicity guarantees that the algorithm will construct the same tree set regardless of how the separation system and tangles to be distinguished are presented to it as input.

Establishing canonical tree-of-tangles theorems has been a long-standing goal in tangle theory, since the original proof in
[
6
]
relied on a technique that is unable to produce canonical results. A first breakthrough towards this goal was achieved in
[
1
]
, which managed to establish such a canonical theorem for tangles in graphs. With a similar overall strategy Diestel, Hundertmark and Lemanczyk
[
3
]
could then extend this canonical result to arbitrary separation systems and the most general class of tangles, which are called
profiles
.

A central ingredient in
[
3
]
is an
order function
on the separations considered, similar to the order
\lvert A\cap B\rvert
of a separation
(A,B)
of a graph that was already used in
[
6
]
and
[
1
]
. In this setting one then considers the separation system
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.76524pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.23444pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{k}}}
of all separations of order less than
k
and studies its tangles. In analogy to the function
{(A,B)\mapsto\lvert A\cap B\rvert}
from graphs this order function is usually assumed to be submodular. This submodularity of the order function has a structural effect on the separation system
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.76524pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.23444pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{k}}}
: for any two separations in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.76524pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.23444pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{k}}}
at least one of their pairwise join and meet (which are separations given by opposite ‘corners’ of these separations) again lies in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.76524pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.23444pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{k}}}
.

Later Diestel, Erde, and Weißauer
[
2
]
showed that the latter structural condition by itself is already sufficiently strong for proving tree-of-tangles theorems: tangle theory can be meaningfully studied without the hitherto usual assumption of a submodular order function, further widening its applicability. If a separation system has this structural property but not necessarily a submodular order function then it is
structurally submodular
or simply
submodular
if the context is clear. The tree-of-tangles theorem established in
[
2
]
then reads as follows:

Theorem 1

(
[
2
]
*Theorem 6)
.

Let
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
be a structurally submodular separation system and
\mathcal{P}
a set of profiles of
S
. Then
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
contains a tree set
N
that distinguishes
\mathcal{P}
.

Here, a tree set
N

distinguishes

\mathcal{P}
if each pair of profiles in
\mathcal{P}
lies on different sides of some separation in
N
. For formal definitions see
Section

2
.

This
1
is even more widely applicable than the result of
[
3
]
, but has one major downside: it does not yield canonicity, since the proof in
[
2
]
chooses certain separations arbitrarily.

In this note we present a new proof, which establishes the following canonical version of
1
:

Theorem 2
.

Let
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
be a structurally submodular separation system and
\mathcal{P}
a set of profiles of
S
. Then there is a nested set
N=N({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\mathcal{P})\subseteq S
which distinguishes
\mathcal{P}
. This
N({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\mathcal{P})
can be chosen canonically: if
\varphi\colon{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\to{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is an isomorphism of separation systems and
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}\coloneqq\left\{\varphi(P)\mid P\in\mathcal{P}\right\}
then
\varphi(N({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\mathcal{P}))=N({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\mathcal{P}^{\prime})
.

There are some technical subtleties in the formulation of
2
due to the fact that neither the profile property nor structural submodularity need be preserved by isomorphisms of separation systems. To avoid these difficulties, we obtain
2
by first establishing the following more general but somewhat more technical result, which slightly weakens the definitions of submodularity and profiles in order to make them compatible with such isomorphisms:

Theorem 3
.

Let
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
be a separation system and
\mathcal{P}
a collection of consistent orientations of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
such that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
\mathcal{P}
-submodular. Then there is a nested set
N=N({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\mathcal{P})\subseteq S
which distinguishes
\mathcal{P}
. This
N({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\mathcal{P})
can be chosen canonically: if
\varphi\colon{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\to{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is an isomorphism of separation systems and
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}\coloneqq\left\{\varphi(P)\mid P\in\mathcal{P}\right\}
then
\varphi(N({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\mathcal{P}))=N({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\mathcal{P}^{\prime})
.

2
is then an immediate corollary of
3
.

This paper is structured as follows: in
Section

2
we recall the relevant definitions of separation systems and profiles. In
Section

3
we introduce the new definition required for
3
and show that
2
indeed is an immediate corollary of
3
. In
Section

4
we prove
2
and
3
.

2
Separation Systems and Profiles

For a full introduction to abstract tangle theory and its terminology and notation we refer the reader to
[
4
,
2
]
. In the remainder of this section we offer a brief introduction of only those terms and notation of tangle theory that are relevant to this paper.

A
separation system

{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}=({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\leq,^{*})
is a poset
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\leq)
together with an order-reversing involution
∗
on
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Given an element
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
we denote its
inverse
, its image under
∗
, by
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}=({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})^{*}
. For
∗
to be order-reversing then means that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
if and only if
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\geq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
for all
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.

We refer to the elements of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
as
oriented separations
. Given such an oriented separation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
we call
\{{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\}
its underlying
unoriented separation
and denote it by
s
. Conversely, given an unoriented separation
s
, we call
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
the two
orientations
of
s=\{{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\}
.

If the context is clear we often refer to both oriented and unoriented separations simply as
separations
. Moreover, when no confusion is possible, we may informally use terms defined for unoriented separations for oriented separations as well and vice-versa. For a set
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of oriented separations we write
S
for the set of all
s
with
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Conversely, if
S
is a set of unoriented separations, we write
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
for the set of all
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
with
s\in S
.

Two separations
r
and
s
are
nested
if they admit orientations
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
with
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. If
r
and
s
are nested we also call all their respective orientations nested. Two separations that are not nested are said to
cross
. Note that two oriented separations
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
need not be themselves comparable in order to be nested: we may have for instance that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. A set of separations is
nested
if its elements are pairwise nested.

We say that two oriented separations
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}

point towards each other
if
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, and that they
point away from each other
if
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Thus
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
are nested if and only if they are either comparable, or point towards each other, or point away from each other.

If
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}=({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\leq,^{*})
is a separation system whose poset is a lattice with pairwise join and meet operations
\lor
and
\land
we call
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\leq,^{*},\lor,\land)
a
universe of separations
. Given
r
and
s
in
U
we call all separations of the form
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
as well as their inverses and underlying separations
corner separations
of
r
and
s
. Note that in universes of separations DeMorgan’s rule holds:

({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})^{*}=({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})

For a universe
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}=({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},\leq,^{*},\lor,\land)
of separations and a separation system
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\subseteq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
we say that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
(structurally) submodular in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
if for all
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
at least one of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is also contained in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.

An
orientation
of a set
S
of unoriented separations is a set
O\subseteq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
containing exactly one orientation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
or
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of each
s=\{{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\}
in
S
. We call
O

consistent
if there are no
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
O
with
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
r\neq s
.

Given a universe
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of separations and a separation system
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\subseteq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, a
profile
of
S
is a consistent orientation of
S
such that

\forall\,{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in P\colon({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})\notin P\,.

This
Section

2
is commonly referred to as the
profile property
.

A separation
s

distinguishes
two profiles
P
and
Q
of
S
if there is an orientation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of
s
with
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in P
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in Q
. A set
N

distinguishes
a set
\mathcal{P}
of profiles of
S
if every pair of distinct profiles in
\mathcal{P}
is distinguished by some
s
in
N
.

A set
N\subseteq S
of unoriented separations is a
tree set
if
N
is nested and there are no
r\neq s\in N
with orientations
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
such that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Note that if
N
is nested and has the property that each separation in
N
distinguishes some pair of profiles of
S
, then
N
is a tree set.

Finally, an
isomorphism
of separation systems
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is a bijective map
\varphi\colon{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\to{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
such that
\varphi({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})=(\varphi({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}))^{*}
for all
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and furthermore
