{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
if and only if
\varphi({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})\leq\varphi({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})
for all
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.

The most prominent occurrence of separation systems is in graphs: given a graph
{G=(V,E)}
, a separation of
G
is a pair
(A,B)
of vertex sets with
A\cup B=V
such that there is no edge from
A\smallsetminus B
to
B\smallsetminus A
. With involution
(A,B)^{*}=(B,A)
and partial order
(A,B)\leq(C,D)
if and only if
A\subseteq C
and
B\supseteq D
, the set of all separations of
G
becomes a separation system – in fact, a universe of separations.

Of special importance in the theory of graph separations are the separation systems
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.76524pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.23444pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{k}}}
of all separations
(A,B)
of
G
with
\lvert A\cap B\rvert<k
. Each such
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 8.13994pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.76524pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.23444pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S_{\mathrlap{k}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{k}}}
constitutes a structurally submodular separation system by our definition. The
k
-tangle of a graph, whose introduction and study by Robertson and Seymour in
[
6
]
was the inception of tangle theory, is then a special type of consistent orientation of
S_{k}
. Each such
k
-tangle is a profile of
S_{k}
in our sense. Profiles, both in graphs and in abstract separation systems, were first rigorously studied in
[
3
]
: that work also contains a plethora of examples and applications of profiles, including an example of a profile in a graph that is not a
k
-tangle (
[
3
]
*Example 7).

The separation systems considered in
[
3
]
, although a generalisation of graph separations, still come with some strong structural assumptions that are closely modelled on graph separations. The first paper to study profiles in universes of separations as defined here, without any additional structural assumptions, was
[
2
]
. That work, too, contains some examples of profiles in separation systems, as well as multiple applications of the theory of tangles and profiles both inside and outside of graph theory. The applications presented in
[
2
]
include profiles in matroids and using tangles for cluster analysis, as well as working with more exotic types of separations such as clique separations in graphs or circle separations outside of graphs.

Both
[
3
]
and
[
2
]
provide their own version of
2
. The version given in
[
3
]
, like our
2
, constructs a
canonical
tree set distinguishing a given set of profiles, but does so by leveraging a much stronger set of assumptions. Conversely, the version
1
of
2
given in
[
2
]
does not yield canonicity.

3
Submodularity with respect to a set of profiles

The first hurdle to overcome when aiming for a canonical version of
1
is to pin down what exactly ‘canonical’ ought to mean. At first glance this is obvious: the construction of the nested set
N
shall use only invariants of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
\mathcal{P}
, that is, properties which are preserved by isomorphisms of separation systems. This approach, however, runs into a subtle difficulty: the definitions of both structural submodularity and profiles depend on
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
being embedded into an ambient universe of separations, whose existence
1
implicitly assumes. An isomorphism
\varphi\colon{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\to{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of separation systems, though, need not preserve such an embedding, which leads to the undesirable situation that a construction isomorphic to that of
N
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
could be carried out in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, even though
2
may not be directly applicable to
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
due to differences in their embeddings into ambient lattices.

To make our canonical version of
1
as widely applicable as possible, and to keep the definition of canonicity as straightforward and clean as possible, we must therefore tweak the assumptions of structural submodularity and profiles of
S
in such a way that they no longer depend on any embedding into a universe of separations, and are themselves invariants of isomorphisms between separation systems. This is made possible by the following observation: the proof of
1
makes use of the assumptions that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is submodular and
\mathcal{P}
a set of profiles solely to deduce that whenever some
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
distinguish some two profiles in
\mathcal{P}
, then either their meet or their join (as provided by the ambient lattice) is contained in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and likewise distinguishes that pair of profiles.

For our canonical
3
we will thus eliminate the need for an ambient universe by asking of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
\mathcal{P}
that they have this property, with the meets or joins now being taken directly in the poset
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Expressed solely in terms of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
\mathcal{P}
, this ‘richness’ property is then preserved by isomorphisms of separation system, independently of any embeddings into lattice structures. This solves the minor problem in the formulation of
2
of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
not meeting the assumption of the theorem despite being isomorphic to a separation system which does.
2
is then obtained as a corollary of
3
.

Let
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
be a separation system and
\mathcal{P}
a set of consistent orientations of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Given a set
M\subseteq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of oriented separations, an element
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is an
infimum
of
M
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
if
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
for each
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in M
and additionally

whenever
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is such that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
for all

. Dually, an element
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is a

of

in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
if
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\geq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}

{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\geq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
for all
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in M
. In general a set
M\subseteq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
need not have such an infimum or supremum in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.

Given two separations
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
we denote the infimum and supremum of
\left\{{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\right\}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
by
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, respectively, if those exist. Observe that
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})^{\ast}={\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.

If
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
have a supremum
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, and every
P\in\mathcal{P}
containing both
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
also contains
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, then we call
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
a
\mathcal{P}
-join
of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Dually we call
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
a
\mathcal{P}
-meet
of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
if
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})^{\ast}\in P
for each
P\in\mathcal{P}
containing both
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.

Finally, we say that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
\mathcal{P}
-submodular
if every two crossing separations
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
have a
\mathcal{P}
-join or
\mathcal{P}
-meet in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. For the remainder of this work we assume
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
to be
\mathcal{P}
-submodular.

Observe that
\mathcal{P}
-submodularity is preserved by isomorphisms of separation systems: if
\varphi\colon{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\to{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is an isomorphism and
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}\coloneqq\left\{\varphi(P)\mid P\in\mathcal{P}\right\}
, then
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
-submodular. Using this notion of submodularity we can therefore meaningfully express canonicity in the context of
1
.

Note, however, that we are not assuming the elements of
\mathcal{P}
to be profiles of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
: this is precisely because we prove
3
without an ambient lattice structure, which would be necessary to define profiles. Therefore,
3
improves on
1
not only by offering a canonical way of constructing
N
, but also by being applicable to an even larger number of separation systems.

Before getting to the proof of
3
itself, let us demonstrate that it is in fact a strengthening of
1
by showing that
3
implies
2
. The following lemma does just that by proving that in the setting of
1
the assumptions of
3
are satisfied:

Lemma 4
.

Let
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
be a structurally submodular separation system inside some universe
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of separations and
\mathcal{P}
a set of profiles of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Then
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
\mathcal{P}
-submodular.

Proof.

We must show that any
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
have a
\mathcal{P}
-meet or
\mathcal{P}
-join. So let
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
be given.
Since
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is structurally submodular it contains the infimum
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
or the supremum
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Let us assume the latter; the other case is dual. Then
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is also the supremum of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
as taken in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Moreover, by the profile property, every
P\in\mathcal{P}
containing both
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
also contains
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, making this a
\mathcal{P}
-join of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.
∎

4
Proof of
2
and
3

In this section we will prove Theorem
2
and Theorem
3
. To this end, for the remainder of this paper let
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
be a separation system and let
\mathcal{P}
be a set of consistent orientations of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
such that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
\mathcal{P}
-submodular.

A common tool in proving tree-of-tangles theorems is the so-called fish lemma
[
4
]
*Lemma 3.2. Since this lemma is usually formulated in the context of a separation system that is contained in a universe of separations, we need to prove our own version of this lemma.

Lemma 5

(See also
[
4
]
*Lemma 3.2)
.

Let
r,s\in S
be two crossing separations in
S
and let
t\in S
be a separation that is nested with both
r
and
s
. Given orientations
