{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of
r
and
s
such that there exists a supremum
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, the separation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is nested with
t
. The same is true for
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.

Proof.

If
t
has an orientation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
such that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
or
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
then clearly
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})
. Otherwise, since
r
and
s
cross, there must be an orientation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of
t
such that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Thus, by the fact that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is the supremum of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, we have that
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.61111pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.02084pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 2.81831pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle t$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.
∎

Let us say that a separation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
exclusive (for
\mathcal{P}
)
if it lies in exactly one orientation in
\mathcal{P}
. If
P\in\mathcal{P}
is the orientation containing an exclusive separation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
then we might also say that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
P
-exclusive (for
\mathcal{P}
)
. Observe that if
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
P
-exclusive for
\mathcal{P}
, then so is every
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in P
with
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.

For each
P\in\mathcal{P}
let
M_{P}
consist of the maximal elements of the set of all
P
-exclusive separations. Equivalently,
M_{P}
is the set of all maximal elements of
P
that are exclusive for
\mathcal{P}
.

Our strategy for proving
3
will be to canonically pick nested
P
-exclusive representatives of all orientations
P\in\mathcal{P}
that contain exclusive separations, then discard from
\mathcal{P}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
all those orientations
P
for whom we selected a representative and all those separations not nested with these representatives, respectively. Iterating this procedure will yield the canonical nested set.

In order for this strategy to work we must ensure that the sets
M_{P}
are not all empty. Our first lemma addresses this:

Lemma 6
.

If
\mathcal{P}
and
S
are non-empty, then some
M_{P}
is non-empty.

In the case of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
being submodular in some universe of separation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 5.54262pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 4.33875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.0mu\hskip 3.68523pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle U$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
\mathcal{P}
being a set of profiles of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, the existence of exclusive separations and thus
6
is actually an immediate consequence of
1
: if
N\subseteq S
is a nested set which distinguishes
\mathcal{P}
, and each element of
N
distinguishes some pair of profiles in
\mathcal{P}
, then any maximal element of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 2.5mu\hskip 7.29997pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle N$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 2.5mu\hskip 7.29997pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle N$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 2.5mu\hskip 5.66779pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle N$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 2.5mu\hskip 4.73892pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle N$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is exclusive for
\mathcal{P}
. In other words, the separations labelling the incoming edges of leaves of the tree associated with
N
are exclusive. (See
[
5
]
for the precise relationship between nested sets and trees.)

However, since we are working with the more general notion of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
being
\mathcal{P}
-submodular, we give an independent proof of
6
.

Proof of
6
.

If
\mathcal{P}
consists of only one orientation the assertion is trivial since
S
is non-empty. For
\lvert\mathcal{P}\rvert\geq 2
we show the following stronger claim by induction on
\lvert\mathcal{P}\rvert
:

If
\lvert\mathcal{P}\rvert\geq 2
there is for each
P\in\mathcal{P}
a separation that is exclusive but not
P
-exclusive for
\mathcal{P}
.

For the base case
\lvert\mathcal{P}\rvert=2
observe that any separation distinguishing the two orientations in
\mathcal{P}
has two exclusive orientations, one in each element of
\mathcal{P}
.

Suppose now that
\lvert\mathcal{P}\rvert>2
and that the claim holds for all non-singleton proper subsets of
\mathcal{P}
. Let
P\in\mathcal{P}
be the given fixed orientation and set
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}\coloneqq\mathcal{P}\smallsetminus\left\{P\right\}
. By the induction hypothesis applied to
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
and an arbitrary orientation in
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
there is an exclusive separation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
for
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
, contained in some
Q\in\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
. Applying the induction hypothesis again to
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
and
Q
yields another separation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
that is exclusive for
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
and lies in some
Q^{\prime}\in\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
with
Q\neq Q^{\prime}
.

If
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
or
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is also exclusive for
\mathcal{P}
then we are done. So suppose not, that is, suppose we have
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}},{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in P
. Then
r\neq s
, and hence
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
must be incomparable by the consistency of
Q
and
Q^{\prime}
. If
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
then
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
Q
-exclusive for
\mathcal{P}
. Moreover
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\leq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is not possible by the consistency of
P
. Thus we may assume that
r
and
s
cross.

By
\mathcal{P}
-submodularity of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, there is a
\mathcal{P}
-join or a
\mathcal{P}
-meet of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
; by symmetry we may assume that there is a
\mathcal{P}
-join
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Since
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
Q^{\prime}
-exclusive we have
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in Q
and hence
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})\in Q
by the fact that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is the
\mathcal{P}
-join of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. From
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})\geq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
we infer that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
Q
-exclusive for
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
. Moreover we cannot have
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})\in P
: it would be inconsistent with
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in P
as
r
and
s
cross.

Therefore
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is exclusive but not
P
-exclusive for
\mathcal{P}
.
∎

We remark that, in the case of a submodular separation system
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
inside a universe of separations and a set
\mathcal{P}
of profiles, the stronger assertion used for the induction hypothesis in this proof, too, can be established immediately using
1
: for
\lvert\mathcal{P}\rvert\geq 2
the tree associated with the nested set
N\subseteq S
distinguishing
\mathcal{P}
has at least two leaves, and hence some leaf for which the separation labelling its incoming edge does not lie in the fixed profile
P
.

Returning to the proof of
3
, let us find a way to canonically pick representatives of those
P\in\mathcal{P}
with non-empty
M_{P}
in such a way that these representatives are nested with each other. For the ‘canonically’-part of this we will make use of the fact that the sets
M_{P}
themselves are invariants of
\mathcal{P}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. For the nestedness we start by showing that separations from different
M_{P}
’s cannot cross at all:

Lemma 7
.

For
P\neq P^{\prime}
all
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in M_{P}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in M_{P^{\prime}}
are pairwise nested.

Proof.

Suppose some
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in M_{P}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in M_{P^{\prime}}
cross. By
\mathcal{P}
-submodularity of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, there is a
\mathcal{P}
-join
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
or a
\mathcal{P}
-meet
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
; by symmetry we may suppose that
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Then
P
, too, contains this separation since
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in P
. But
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is also
P
-exclusive and strictly larger than
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, a contradiction.
∎

It is possible, however, that the set
M_{P}
itself is not nested. In fact the elements of
M_{P}
all cross each other, unless
\mathcal{P}=\left\{P\right\}
: any
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
M_{P}
that are nested must point towards each other by maximality. But every other orientation in
\mathcal{P}
contains both
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and would then be inconsistent. If we want to represent a
P\in\mathcal{P}
with non-empty
M_{P}
by an element of
M_{P}
, we are therefore limited to picking at most one element of
M_{P}
. However there is no canonical way of singling out an element of
M_{P}
to be the representative of
P
; we must therefore find another way of choosing an invariant
P
-exclusive separation, using
M_{P}
only as a starting point.

For this we will show that each
M_{P}
has an infimum in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and that this infimum is again
P
-exclusive:

Lemma 8
.

Let
P\in\mathcal{P}
with
M_{P}\neq\emptyset
and
\mathcal{P}\neq\left\{P\right\}
be given. Then
M_{P}
has an infimum
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 11.35521pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 11.35521pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.51917pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.09557pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{P}}}
in the poset
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 11.35521pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 11.35521pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.51917pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.09557pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{P}}}
is
P
-exclusive for
\mathcal{P}
. Moreover if some
t\in S
is nested with
M_{P}
then
t
is also nested with
s_{P}
.

Proof.

Fix an enumeration
M_{P}=\left\{{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.83109pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.43297pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{1}}},\dots,{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 10.2327pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{n}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 10.2327pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{n}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.83224pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{n}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.43413pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{n}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{n}}}\right\}
and some
t\in S
that is nested with
M_{P}
. We will show by induction on
i
that there is an infimum
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.94217pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.51857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}\coloneqq\inf\{{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.83109pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.43297pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{1}}},\dots,{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}\}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
; that this infimum is
P
-exclusive for
\mathcal{P}
; and that it is nested with
t
. This then yields the claim for
i=n
.

The case
i=1
is trivially true, so suppose that
i>1
and that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}=\inf\{{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.83109pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.43297pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{1}}},\dots,{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.79924pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.79924pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.86008pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.46196pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}\}
is already known to be the infimum of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.83109pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.43297pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{1}}},\dots,{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.79924pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.79924pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.86008pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.46196pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, that it is
P
-exclusive, and that it is nested with
t
.

In the case that
s_{i-1}=r_{i}
we have either
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}={\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
or
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}={\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
. The latter of these is impossible since
P
contains both of the
P
-exclusive separations
