{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
. The former, however, gives that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
is the infimum of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.83109pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.43297pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{1}}},\dots,{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
and thus as claimed by
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}\in M_{P}
.

So suppose that
s_{i-1}\neq r_{i}
. Let us first treat the case that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
are nested. Clearly the two cannot point away from each other since
P
is consistent. If
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
are comparable then one of the two is the infimum of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
and thus the infimum of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.2755pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.83109pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.43297pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{1}}},\dots,{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Since both
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
are
P
-exclusive and nested with
t
, this infimum is thus as claimed. Finally, if
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
point towards each other, we obtain a contradiction: for then their inverses point away from each other, making every orientation in
\mathcal{P}
other than
P
inconsistent. Thus if
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
are nested the induction hypothesis holds for
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.94217pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.51857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
.

Let us now consider the case that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
cross. Then there needs to be a
\mathcal{P}
-join or a
\mathcal{P}
-meet of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
. However we cannot have a
\mathcal{P}
-join
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
since this join would be
P
-exclusive and strictly larger than
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}\in M_{P}
. Therefore there is a
\mathcal{P}
-meet
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}})\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. By consistency we have that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.94217pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.51857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}\in P
. Every orientation in
\mathcal{P}
other than
P
contains
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.11865pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.0961pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.69798pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
as well as
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 14.69737pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.70615pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 12.28255pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i-1}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i-1}}}
and hence
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.94217pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.51857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
by the definition of
\mathcal{P}
-meet, which shows that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.94217pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.51857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
is
P
-exclusive. Finally, by
5
,
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 8.01678pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.94217pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.51857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{i}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{i}}}
is also nested with
t
.
∎

It remains to show that after picking as a representative for each
P\in\mathcal{P}
with exclusive separations the infimum of
M_{P}
, the set of separations in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
that are nested with all these representatives is still rich enough to distinguish all orientations in
\mathcal{P}
for which we have not yet picked a representative.

For this let
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\subseteq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
be the system of all those separations that are nested with all
M_{P}
, and let
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}\subseteq\mathcal{P}
be the set of those orientations
Q
that have empty
M_{Q}
. Our next lemma says that if we restrict ourselves to
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, we can still distinguish
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
:

Lemma 9
.

The separation system
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
-submodular and distinguishes
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
.

Proof.

The fact that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
-submodular is a direct consequence of
5
: it implies that for
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
any
\mathcal{P}
-meet or
\mathcal{P}
-join of them in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is contained in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Since
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}\subseteq\mathcal{P}
this
\mathcal{P}
-join or
\mathcal{P}
-meet is in fact a
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
-join or
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
-meet of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.6875pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.77432pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\;$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.

To see that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
distinguishes
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
, let
Q
and
Q^{\prime}
be distinct orientations in
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
; we shall show that some
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
distinguishes them. For this choose a separation
s\in S
which distinguishes
Q
and
Q^{\prime}
and which is nested with
M_{P}
for as many
P\in\mathcal{P}
as possible. If
s
is nested with all
M_{P}
we are done; otherwise there is some
P\in\mathcal{P}
for which
s
crosses some separation in
M_{P}
.

So suppose that there is a
P\in\mathcal{P}
for which
s
is not nested with
M_{P}
. Among all
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
which distinguish
Q
and
Q^{\prime}
and which are nested with each
M_{P^{\prime}}
with which
s
is nested, pick a minimal
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
with
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in P
. We claim that this
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is nested with
M_{P}
, contradicting the choice of
s
.

To see this, suppose that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
crosses some
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\in M_{P}
. Then there cannot exist a
\mathcal{P}
-join of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
since this join would be a strictly larger
P
-exclusive separation than
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. Hence there is a
\mathcal{P}
-meet
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
. By
P\notin\left\{Q,Q^{\prime}\right\}
we have that both
Q
and
Q^{\prime}
contain
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, and hence this separation
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
distinguishes
Q
and
Q^{\prime}
as well: one of the two orientations contains
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and thus also
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
by consistency. The other contains both
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and thus also
({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\lor{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})=({\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}})^{\ast}
by the fact that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is the
\mathcal{P}
-meet of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
.
However, by
5
and
7
, this
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 4.78937pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.92825pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 3.53014pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle r$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\land{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
would be nested with each
M_{P^{\prime}}
with which
s
was nested, while being strictly smaller than
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 7.39238pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.4792pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 6.0556pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, a contradiction.
∎

If
M_{P}
is non-empty let us write
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 11.35521pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 11.35521pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.51917pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.09557pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{P}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{P}}}
for its infimum in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
as in
8
. We are now ready to prove
3
by induction.

Proof of
3
.

We proceed by induction on
\lvert\mathcal{P}\rvert
. If
\lvert\mathcal{P}\rvert\leq 1
there is nothing to show, so suppose that
\lvert\mathcal{P}\rvert>1
and that the assertion holds for all proper subsets of
\mathcal{P}
.

Recall that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}\subseteq{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
consists of all separations in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
that are nested with all sets
M_{Q}
and that
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}\subseteq\mathcal{P}
is the set of all
Q\in\mathcal{P}
with empty
M_{Q}
. Clearly both
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
are invariants of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
\mathcal{P}
since the sets
M_{Q}
themselves are invariants. For each non-empty
M_{Q}
let
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 11.48528pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle s_{\mathrlap{Q}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 11.48528pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle s_{\mathrlap{Q}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.68549pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{Q}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 0.0mu\hskip 9.26189pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle s_{\mathrlap{Q}}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}\hphantom{{}_{Q}}}
be its infimum in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
as described in
8
. Then

N_{1}\coloneqq\left\{s_{Q}\mid Q\in\mathcal{P}\smallsetminus\mathcal{P}^{\prime}\right\}

is clearly a canonical set. From
8
we further know that
N_{1}
distinguishes all orientations in
\mathcal{P}\smallsetminus\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
from each other and from each orientation in
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
.

By
7
every element of
M_{P}
is nested with every element of
M_{P^{\prime}}
for all
P\neq P^{\prime}
. Applying the ‘moreover’-part of
8
twice thus implies that
s_{P}
is nested with every element of
M_{P^{\prime}}
and subsequently with
s_{P^{\prime}}
. Therefore
N_{1}
is a nested set. Likewise every separation in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is nested with
N_{1}
.

Let us apply the induction hypothesis to
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
in
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
, as made possible by
6
and
9
, yielding a canonical nested set
N_{2}\subseteq S^{\prime}
which distinguishes
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
. Since
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 6.58923pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 5.59857pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\enskip$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S^{\scriptscriptstyle\prime}$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
\mathcal{P}^{\prime}
themselves are invariants of
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and
\mathcal{P}
we have that the union
N_{1}\cup N_{2}
is the desired canonical nested set.
∎

Proof of
2
.

By
4
, given a structurally submodular separation system
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
and a set
\mathcal{P}
of profiles of
S
, we know that
{\mathchoice{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\displaystyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\displaystyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\tf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\textstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 4.69582pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\textstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\sf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.70515pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}{\ssf@size pt/10\vbox{\halign{#\cr\hskip 0.0pt$\scriptscriptstyle\mskip 3.0mu\hskip 3.17433pt$\tex_cr:D\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S$}\tex_cr:D}\par}}}
is
\mathcal{P}
-submodular. Thus
2
follows from
3
.
∎
